# Israeli audio: Hebrew, English, and phonetics

Read the Hebrew **right to left: ←**. Its English meaning is below it; the phonetic reading is below the English. Click a filename to open that audio file.

The stable filenames and stable audio IDs remain unchanged. `m` is Ari; `f` is Noa. The same word is listed once per voice recording.

**Words:** 3518 clips. **Reading lines:** 580 clips. English and phonetic labels are also available in [INDEX.csv](INDEX.csv) for every produced clip in the branch.

Phonetics use the project’s siddur-style Latin transliteration (for example, `ch` for the throat sound, `kh` for the similar guttural sound, `tz` for צ, and `sh` for שׁ). Hebrew pronunciation is authoritative; Latin spelling is a learner aid.

English is aligned from the project’s vocabulary, phrasebook and corpus translations. A compound Hebrew word can include an attached particle (for example, “and…”, “to…” or “the…”). `the LORD` represents the divine name according to the app’s reading convention.

| [adon-olam-1-l1](f/israeli/line/adon-olam-1-l1.mp3) | אֲדוֹן עוֹלָם אֲשֶׁר מָלַךְ בְּטֶרֶם כָּל יְצִיר נִבְרָא׃ | Lord of the world, who reigned before any form was created. | adon olam asher malakh beterem kol yetzir nivra |
| [adon-olam-1-l10](f/israeli/line/adon-olam-1-l10.mp3) | וְעִם רוּחִי גְּוִיָּתִי אֲדֹנָי לִי וְלֹא אִירָא׃ | And with my spirit, my body too: the LORD is with me and I will not fear. | ve'im ruchi geviyati adonai li velo ira |
| [adon-olam-1-l2](f/israeli/line/adon-olam-1-l2.mp3) | לְעֵת נַעֲשָׂה בְחֶפְצוֹ כֹּל אֲזַי מֶלֶךְ שְׁמוֹ נִקְרָא׃ | At the time when all was made by his will, then "King" was his name proclaimed. | le'et na'asah vecheftzo kol azai melekh shemo nikra |
| [adon-olam-1-l3](f/israeli/line/adon-olam-1-l3.mp3) | וְאַחֲרֵי כִּכְלוֹת הַכֹּל לְבַדּוֹ יִמְלֹךְ נוֹרָא׃ | And after all things have ended, he alone, the awesome one, will reign. | ve'acharei kikhlot hakol levado yimlokh nora |
| [adon-olam-1-l4](f/israeli/line/adon-olam-1-l4.mp3) | וְהוּא הָיָה וְהוּא הֹוֶה וְהוּא יִהְיֶה בְּתִפְאָרָה׃ | And he was, and he is, and he will be, in glory. | vehu hayah vehu hoveh vehu yihyeh betif'arah |
| [adon-olam-1-l5](f/israeli/line/adon-olam-1-l5.mp3) | וְהוּא אֶחָד וְאֵין שֵׁנִי לְהַמְשִׁיל לוֹ לְהַחְבִּירָה׃ | And he is one, and there is no second, to compare to him or to join with him. | vehu echad ve'ein sheni lehamshil lo lehachbirah |
| [adon-olam-1-l6](f/israeli/line/adon-olam-1-l6.mp3) | בְּלִי רֵאשִׁית בְּלִי תַכְלִית וְלוֹ הָעֹז וְהַמִּשְׂרָה׃ | Without beginning, without end; to him belong the power and the dominion. | beli reshit beli takhlit velo ha'oz vehamisrah |
| [adon-olam-1-l7](f/israeli/line/adon-olam-1-l7.mp3) | וְהוּא אֵלִי וְחַי גוֹאֲלִי וְצוּר חֶבְלִי בְּעֵת צָרָה׃ | And he is my God, and my living redeemer, and the rock of my portion in time of distress. | vehu eli vechai go'ali vetzur chevli be'et tzarah |
| [adon-olam-1-l8](f/israeli/line/adon-olam-1-l8.mp3) | וְהוּא נִסִּי וּמָנוֹס לִי מְנָת כּוֹסִי בְּיוֹם אֶקְרָא׃ | And he is my banner and my refuge, the portion of my cup on the day I call. | vehu nisi umanos li menat kosi beyom ekra |

| [aleinu-1](f/israeli/line/aleinu-1.mp3) | עָלֵינוּ לְשַׁבֵּחַ לַאֲדוֹן הַכֹּל לָתֵת גְּדֻלָּה לְיוֹצֵר בְּרֵאשִׁית שֶׁלֹּא עָשָׂנוּ כְּגוֹיֵי הָאֲרָצוֹת וְלֹא שָׂמָנוּ כְּמִשְׁפְּחוֹת הָאֲדָמָה שֶׁלֹּא שָׂם חֶלְקֵנוּ כָּהֶם וְגוֹרָלֵנוּ כְּכָל־הֲמוֹנָם שֶׁהֵם מִשְׁתַּחֲוִים לָהֶבֶל וָרִיק וּמִתְפַּלְּלִים אֶל־אֵל לֹא יוֹשִׁיעַ וַאֲנַחְנוּ מִשְׁתַּחֲוִים לִפְנֵי מֶלֶךְ מַלְכֵי הַמְּלָכִים הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא שֶׁהוּא נוֹטֶה שָׁמַיִם וְיוֹסֵד אָרֶץ וּמוֹשַׁב יְקָרוֹ בַּשָּׁמַיִם מִמַּעַל וּשְׁכִינַת עֻזּוֹ בְּגָבְהֵי מְרוֹמִים הוּא אֱלֹהֵינוּ וְאֵין עוֹד אַחֵר אֱמֶת מַלְכֵּנוּ וְאֶפֶס זוּלָתוֹ כַּכָּתוּב בַּתּוֹרָה וְיָדַעְתָּ הַיּוֹם וַהֲשֵׁבֹתָ אֶל־לְבָבֶךָ כִּי אֲדֹנָי הוּא הָאֱלֹהִים בַּשָּׁמַיִם מִמַּעַל וְעַל־הָאָרֶץ מִתָּחַת אֵין עוֹד׃ | It is our duty to praise the Master of all, to ascribe greatness to the Creator of the beginning: that he has not made us like the nations of the lands and has not placed us like the families of the earth, that he has not set our portion like theirs nor our lot like all their multitude. For they bow to vanity and emptiness and pray to a god who does not save; but we bow before the King of kings of kings, the Holy One, blessed be he, who stretches out the heavens and founds the earth, whose seat of glory is in the heavens above and the presence of his might in the loftiest heights. He is our God; there is no other. Truly he is our king; there is nothing besides him. As it is written in the Torah: "You shall know this day and take it to heart that the LORD is God, in the heavens above and on the earth beneath; there is no other." | aleinu leshabeach la'adon hakol latet gedulah leyotzer bereshit shelo asanu kegoyei ha'aratzot velo samanu kemishpechot ha'adamah shelo sam chelkenu kahem vegoralenu kekhol-hamonam shehem mishtachavim lahevel varik umitpalelim el-el lo yoshi'a va'anachnu mishtachavim lifnei melekh malkhei hamelakhim hakadosh barukh hu shehu noteh shamayim veyosed aretz umoshav yekaro bashamayim mima'al ushekhinat uzo begavehei meromim hu eloheinu ve'ein od acher emet malkenu ve'efes zulato kakatuv batorah veyadata hayom vahashevota el-levavekha ki adonai hu ha'elohim bashamayim mima'al ve'al-ha'aretz mitachat ein od |
| [amidah-elohai-netzor](f/israeli/line/amidah-elohai-netzor.mp3) | אֱלֹהַי נְצוֹר לְשׁוֹנִי מֵרָע וּשְׂפָתַי מִדַּבֵּר מִרְמָה וְלִמְקַלְלַי נַפְשִׁי תִדּוֹם וְנַפְשִׁי כֶּעָפָר לַכֹּל תִּהְיֶה פְּתַח לִבִּי בְּתוֹרָתֶךָ וּבְמִצְוֹתֶיךָ תִּרְדֹּף נַפְשִׁי וְכֹל הַחוֹשְׁבִים עָלַי רָעָה מְהֵרָה הָפֵר עֲצָתָם וְקַלְקֵל מַחֲשַׁבְתָּם׃ עֲשֵׂה לְמַעַן שְׁמֶךָ עֲשֵׂה לְמַעַן יְמִינֶךָ עֲשֵׂה לְמַעַן קְדֻשָּׁתֶךָ עֲשֵׂה לְמַעַן תּוֹרָתֶךָ לְמַעַן יֵחָלְצוּן יְדִידֶיךָ הוֹשִׁיעָה יְמִינְךָ וַעֲנֵנִי׃ יִהְיוּ לְרָצוֹן אִמְרֵי פִי וְהֶגְיוֹן לִבִּי לְפָנֶיךָ אֲדֹנָי צוּרִי וְגוֹאֲלִי׃ | My God, guard my tongue from evil and my lips from speaking deceit. To those who curse me, let my soul be silent; let my soul be like dust to all. Open my heart to your Torah, and let my soul pursue your commandments. As for all who plan evil against me, quickly annul their counsel and frustrate their plans. Act for the sake of your name; act for the sake of your right hand; act for the sake of your holiness; act for the sake of your Torah. That your beloved may be rescued, save with your right hand and answer me. May the words of my mouth and the thoughts of my heart be acceptable before you, LORD, my rock and my redeemer. | elohai netzor leshoni mera usefatai midaber mirmah velimkalelai nafshi tidom venafshi ke'afar lakol tihyeh petach libi betoratekha uvemitzvotekha tirdof nafshi vekhol hachoshevim alai ra'ah meherah hafer atzatam vekalkel machashavtam aseh lema'an shemekha aseh lema'an yeminekha aseh lema'an kedushatekha aseh lema'an toratekha lema'an yechaltzun yedidekha hoshi'ah yeminekha va'aneni yihyu leratzon imrei fi vehegyon libi lefanekha adonai tzuri vego'ali |

| [brich-shmei](f/israeli/line/brich-shmei.mp3) | בְּרִיךְ שְׁמֵהּ דְּמָרֵא עָלְמָא בְּרִיךְ כִּתְרָךְ וְאַתְרָךְ יְהֵא רְעוּתָךְ עִם עַמָּךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל לְעָלַם וּפֻרְקַן יְמִינָךְ אַחֲזֵי לְעַמָּךְ בְּבֵית מַקְדְּשָׁךְ וּלְאַמְטוּיֵי לָנָא מִטּוּב נְהוֹרָךְ וּלְקַבֵּל צְלוֹתָנָא בְּרַחֲמִין יְהֵא רַעֲוָא קֳדָמָךְ דְּתוֹרִיךְ לָן חַיִּין בְּטִיבוּ וְלֶהֱוֵי אֲנָא פְקִידָא בְּגוֹ צַדִּיקַיָּא לְמִרְחַם עָלַי וּלְמִנְטַר יָתִי וְיַת־כָּל־דִּי־לִי וְדִי־לְעַמָּךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל אַנְתְּ הוּא זָן לְכֹלָּא וּמְפַרְנֵס לְכֹלָּא אַנְתְּ הוּא שַׁלִּיט עַל־כֹּלָּא אַנְתְּ הוּא דְּשַׁלִּיט עַל־מַלְכַיָּא וּמַלְכוּתָא דִּילָךְ הִיא אֲנָא עַבְדָּא דְקֻדְשָׁא בְּרִיךְ הוּא דְּסָגִידְנָא קַמֵּהּ וּמִקַּמֵּי דִּיקַר אוֹרַיְתֵהּ בְּכָל־עִדָּן וְעִדָּן לָא עַל־אֱנָשׁ רָחִיצְנָא וְלָא עַל־בַּר־אֱלָהִין סָמִיכְנָא אֶלָּא בֵּאלָהָא דִשְׁמַיָּא דְּהוּא אֱלָהָא קְשׁוֹט וְאוֹרַיְתֵהּ קְשׁוֹט וּנְבִיאוֹהִי קְשׁוֹט וּמַסְגֵּא לְמֶעְבַּד טָבְוָן וּקְשׁוֹט בֵּהּ אֲנָא רָחִיץ וְלִשְׁמֵהּ קַדִּישָׁא יַקִּירָא אֲנָא אֵמַר תֻּשְׁבְּחָן יְהֵא רַעֲוָא קֳדָמָךְ דְּתִפְתַּח לִבָּאי בְּאוֹרַיְתָא וְתַשְׁלִים מִשְׁאֲלִין דְּלִבָּאי וְלִבָּא דְכָל־עַמָּךְ יִשְׂרָאֵל לְטָב וּלְחַיִּין וְלִשְׁלָם׃ | Blessed is the name of the Master of the world; blessed is your crown and your place. May your good will be with your people Israel for ever; show your people the redemption of your right hand in your Temple, bring us of the goodness of your light, and accept our prayer in mercy. May it be your will to lengthen our life in goodness, and let me be counted among the righteous, so that you have mercy on me and guard me and all that is mine and your people Israel's. You feed and sustain all; you rule over all; you rule over kings, and the kingdom is yours. I am a servant of the Holy One, blessed be he, before whom and before whose glorious Torah I bow at every time. Not in any person do I trust, and not on any angel do I rely, but on the God of heaven, who is the God of truth, whose Torah is truth and whose prophets are truth, and who abounds in deeds of goodness and truth. In him I trust, and to his holy and glorious name I utter praises. May it be your will to open my heart to Torah, to fulfil the requests of my heart and the heart of all your people Israel, for good, for life and for peace. | berikh shemeh demare alema berikh kitrakh ve'atrakh yehe re'utakh im amakh yisra'el le'alam ufurkan yeminakh achazei le'amakh beveit makdeshakh ule'amtuyei lana mituv nehorakh ulekabel tzelotana berachamin yehe ra'ava kodamakh detorikh lan chayin betivu velehevei ana fekida bego tzadikaya lemircham alai ulemintar yati veyat-kol-di-li vedi-le'amakh yisra'el ant hu zan lekhola umefarnes lekhola ant hu shalit al-kola ant hu deshalit al-malkhaya umalkhuta dilakh hi ana avda dekudsha berikh hu desagidena kameh umikamei dikar orayteh bekhol-idan ve'idan la al-enash rachitzena vela al-bar-elahin samikhena ela belaha dishmaya dehu elaha keshot ve'orayteh keshot unevi'ohi keshot umasge lemebad tavevan ukeshot beh ana rachitz velishmeh kadisha yakira ana emar tushbechan yehe ra'ava kodamakh detiftach libay be'orayta vetashlim mish'alin delibay veliba dekhal-amakh yisra'el letav ulechayin velishlam |

| [haftarah-after-l1](f/israeli/line/haftarah-after-l1.mp3) | בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם צוּר כָּל־הָעוֹלָמִים צַדִּיק בְּכָל־הַדּוֹרוֹת הָאֵל הַנֶּאֱמָן הָאֹמֵר וְעֹשֶׂה הַמְדַבֵּר וּמְקַיֵּם שֶׁכָּל־דְּבָרָיו אֱמֶת וָצֶדֶק נֶאֱמָן אַתָּה הוּא אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵינוּ וְנֶאֱמָנִים דְּבָרֶיךָ וְדָבָר אֶחָד מִדְּבָרֶיךָ אָחוֹר לֹא יָשׁוּב רֵיקָם כִּי אֵל מֶלֶךְ נֶאֱמָן וְרַחֲמָן אָתָּה בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה אֲדֹנָי הָאֵל הַנֶּאֱמָן בְּכָל־דְּבָרָיו׃ | Blessed are you, LORD our God, king of the world, rock of all the worlds, righteous in all generations, the faithful God who says and does, who speaks and fulfils, all of whose words are true and just. You are faithful, LORD our God, and your words are faithful, and not one of your words returns empty, for you are a faithful and merciful God and king. Blessed are you, LORD, the God who is faithful in all his words. | barukh atah adonai eloheinu melekh ha'olam tzur kol-ha'olamim tzadik bekhol-hadorot ha'el hane'eman ha'omer ve'oseh hamdaber umekayem shekol-devarav emet vatzedek ne'eman atah hu adonai eloheinu vene'emanim devarekha vedavar echad midevarekha achor lo yashuv reikam ki el melekh ne'eman verachaman atah barukh atah adonai ha'el hane'eman bekhol-devarav |
| [haftarah-after-l2](f/israeli/line/haftarah-after-l2.mp3) | רַחֵם עַל צִיּוֹן כִּי הִיא בֵּית חַיֵּינוּ וְלַעֲלוּבַת נֶפֶשׁ תּוֹשִׁיעַ בִּמְהֵרָה בְיָמֵינוּ בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה אֲדֹנָי מְשַׂמֵּחַ צִיּוֹן בְּבָנֶיהָ׃ | Have mercy on Zion, for it is the home of our life, and save the one with a grieving soul quickly in our days. Blessed are you, LORD, who gladdens Zion through her children. | rachem al tziyon ki hi beit chayeinu vela'aluvat nefesh toshi'a bimherah veyameinu barukh atah adonai mesameach tziyon bevaneha |
| [haftarah-after-l3](f/israeli/line/haftarah-after-l3.mp3) | שַׂמְּחֵנוּ אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵינוּ בְּאֵלִיָהוּ הַנָּבִיא עַבְדָּךְ וּבְמַלְכוּת בֵּית דָּוִד מְשִׁיחָךְ בִּמְהֵרָה יָבֹא וְיָגֵל לִבֵּנוּ עַל כִּסְאוֹ לֹא יֵשֵׁב זָר וְלֹא יִנְחֲלוּ עוֹד אֲחֵרִים אֶת כְּבוֹדוֹ כִּי בְשֵׁם קָדְשְׁךָ נִשְׁבַּעְתָּ לּוֹ שֶׁלֹּא יִכְבֶּה נֵרוֹ לְעוֹלָם וָעֶד בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה אֲדֹנָי מָגֵן דָּוִד׃ | Gladden us, LORD our God, with Elijah the prophet your servant and with the kingdom of the house of David your anointed; may he come quickly and make our heart rejoice. On his throne let no stranger sit, and let others not inherit his glory any more, for by your holy name you swore to him that his light would never go out. Blessed are you, LORD, shield of David. | samechenu adonai eloheinu be'eliyahu hanavi avdakh uvemalkhut beit david meshichakh bimherah yavo veyagel libenu al kis'o lo yeshev zar velo yinchalu od acherim et kevodo ki veshem kadshekha nishbata lo shelo yikhbeh nero le'olam va'ed barukh atah adonai magen david |
| [haftarah-after-l4](f/israeli/line/haftarah-after-l4.mp3) | עַל הַתּוֹרָה וְעַל הָעֲבוֹדָה וְעַל הַנְּבִיאִים וְעַל יוֹם הַשַּׁבָּת הַזֶּה שֶׁנָּתַתָּ לָּנוּ אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵינוּ לִקְדֻשָּׁה וְלִמְנוּחָה לְכָבוֹד וּלְתִפְאָרֶת עַל הַכֹּל אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵינוּ אֲנַחְנוּ מוֹדִים לָךְ וּמְבָרְכִים אוֹתָךְ יִתְבָּרַךְ שִׁמְךָ בְּפִי כָּל־חַי תָּמִיד לְעוֹלָם וָעֶד בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה אֲדֹנָי מְקַדֵּשׁ הַשַּׁבָּת אָמֵן׃ | For the Torah, and for the service, and for the prophets, and for this Shabbat day which you gave us, LORD our God, for holiness and rest, for honour and glory. For all this, LORD our God, we thank you and bless you. May your name be blessed by every living being, always, for ever. Blessed are you, LORD, who makes the Shabbat holy. | al hatorah ve'al ha'avodah ve'al hanevi'im ve'al yom hashabat hazeh shenatata lanu adonai eloheinu likdushah velimnuchah lekhavod uletif'aret al hakol adonai eloheinu anachnu modim lakh umevarekhim otakh yitbarakh shimkha befi kol-chai tamid le'olam va'ed barukh atah adonai mekadesh hashabat amen |
| [haftarah-before](f/israeli/line/haftarah-before.mp3) | בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם אֲשֶׁר בָּחַר בִּנְבִיאִים טוֹבִים וְרָצָה בְדִבְרֵיהֶם הַנֶּאֱמָרִים בֶּאֱמֶת בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה אֲדֹנָי הַבּוֹחֵר בַּתּוֹרָה וּבְמֹשֶׁה עַבְדּוֹ וּבְיִשְׂרָאֵל עַמּוֹ וּבִנְבִיאֵי הָאֱמֶת וְהַצֶּדֶק׃ | Blessed are you, LORD our God, king of the world, who chose good prophets and was pleased with their words, which were spoken in truth. Blessed are you, LORD, who chooses the Torah, and Moses his servant, and Israel his people, and prophets of truth and righteousness. | barukh atah adonai eloheinu melekh ha'olam asher bachar binvi'im tovim veratzah vedivreihem hane'emarim be'emet barukh atah adonai habocher batorah uvemosheh avdo uveyisra'el amo uvinvi'ei ha'emet vehatzedek |
| [hagomel-l1](f/israeli/line/hagomel-l1.mp3) | בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם הַגּוֹמֵל לְחַיָּבִים טוֹבוֹת שֶׁגְּמָלַנִי כָּל טוֹב׃ | Blessed are you, LORD our God, king of the world, who bestows good things on the undeserving, and has bestowed every good on me. | barukh atah adonai eloheinu melekh ha'olam hagomel lechayavim tovot shegemalani kol tov |
| [hagomel-l2](f/israeli/line/hagomel-l2.mp3) | מִי שֶׁגְּמָלְךָ טוֹב הוּא יִגְמָלְךָ כָּל טוֹב סֶלָה׃ | May the one who has bestowed good on you bestow on you every good. Selah. | mi shegemalekha tov hu yigmalekha kol tov selah |
| [hamotzi](f/israeli/line/hamotzi.mp3) | בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם הַמּוֹצִיא לֶחֶם מִן הָאָרֶץ׃ | Blessed are you, LORD our God, king of the world, who brings forth bread from the earth. | barukh atah adonai eloheinu melekh ha'olam hamotzi lechem min ha'aretz |
| [hanerot-halalu](f/israeli/line/hanerot-halalu.mp3) | הַנֵּרוֹת הַלָּלוּ אֲנַחְנוּ מַדְלִיקִין עַל הַנִּסִּים וְעַל הַתְּשׁוּעוֹת וְעַל הַנִּפְלָאוֹת שֶׁעָשִׂיתָ לַאֲבוֹתֵינוּ עַל יְדֵי כֹּהֲנֶיךָ הַקְּדוֹשִׁים וְכָל־שְׁמוֹנַת יְמֵי חֲנֻכָּה הַנֵּרוֹת הַלָּלוּ קֹדֶשׁ וְאֵין לָנוּ רְשׁוּת לְהִשְׁתַּמֵּשׁ בָּהֶם אֶלָּא לִרְאוֹתָם בִּלְבָד כְּדֵי לְהוֹדוֹת לִשְׁמֶךָ עַל נִסֶּיךָ נִפְלְאוֹתֶיךָ וִישׁוּעוֹתֶיךָ׃ | We light these lights for the miracles, the deliverances, and the wonders that you did for our ancestors through your holy priests. Throughout the eight days of Hanukkah these lights are holy, and we may not use them, only look at them, in order to thank and praise your name for your miracles, your wonders and your salvations. | hanerot halalu anachnu madlikin al hanisim ve'al hateshu'ot ve'al hanifla'ot she'asita la'avoteinu al yedei kohanekha hakedoshim vekhol-shemonat yemei chanukah hanerot halalu kodesh ve'ein lanu reshut lehishtamesh bahem ela lir'otam bilvad kedei lehodot lishmekha al nisekha nifle'otekha vishu'otekha |
| [hanukkah-candle](f/israeli/line/hanukkah-candle.mp3) | בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם אֲשֶׁר קִדְּשָׁנוּ בְּמִצְוֹתָיו וְצִוָּנוּ לְהַדְלִיק נֵר חֲנֻכָּה׃ | Blessed are you, LORD our God, king of the world, who made us holy with his commandments and commanded us to light the Hanukkah light. | barukh atah adonai eloheinu melekh ha'olam asher kideshanu bemitzvotav vetzivanu lehadlik ner chanukah |
| [hanukkah-nissim](f/israeli/line/hanukkah-nissim.mp3) | בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם שֶׁעָשָׂה נִסִּים לַאֲבוֹתֵינוּ בַּיָּמִים הָהֵם בַּזְּמַן הַזֶּה׃ | Blessed are you, LORD our God, king of the world, who performed miracles for our ancestors in those days, at this time. | barukh atah adonai eloheinu melekh ha'olam she'asah nisim la'avoteinu bayamim hahem bazeman hazeh |
| [hashivenu](f/israeli/line/hashivenu.mp3) | הֲשִׁיבֵנוּ אֲדֹנָי אֵלֶיךָ וְנָשׁוּבָה חַדֵּשׁ יָמֵינוּ כְּקֶדֶם׃ | Turn Thou us unto Thee, O LORD, and we shall be turned; Renew our days as of old. | hashivenu adonai elekha venashuvah chadesh yameinu kekedem |
| [havdalah-flame](f/israeli/line/havdalah-flame.mp3) | בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם בּוֹרֵא מְאוֹרֵי הָאֵשׁ׃ | Blessed are you, LORD our God, king of the world, who creates the lights of the fire. | barukh atah adonai eloheinu melekh ha'olam bore me'orei ha'esh |
| [havdalah-hamavdil](f/israeli/line/havdalah-hamavdil.mp3) | בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם הַמַּבְדִּיל בֵּין קֹדֶשׁ לְחוֹל וּבֵין אוֹר לְחֹשֶׁךְ וּבֵין יִשְׂרָאֵל לָעַמִּים וּבֵין יוֹם הַשְּׁבִיעִי לְשֵׁשֶׁת יְמֵי הַמַּעֲשֶׂה בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה אֲדֹנָי הַמַּבְדִּיל בֵּין קֹדֶשׁ לְחוֹל׃ | Blessed are you, LORD our God, king of the world, who separates between holy and ordinary, between light and darkness, between Israel and the nations, between the seventh day and the six days of work. | [havdalah-opening](f/israeli/line/havdalah-opening.mp3) | הִנֵּה אֵל יְשׁוּעָתִי אֶבְטַח וְלֹא אֶפְחָד׃ כִּי עָזִּי וְזִמְרָת יָהּ אֲדֹנָי וַיְהִי לִי לִישׁוּעָה׃ וּשְׁאַבְתֶּם מַיִם בְּשָׂשׁוֹן מִמַּעַיְנֵי הַיְשׁוּעָה׃ לַאֲדֹנָי הַיְשׁוּעָה עַל עַמְּךָ בִרְכָתֶךָ סֶּלָה׃ אֲדֹנָי צְבָאוֹת עִמָּנוּ מִשְׂגָּב לָנוּ אֱלֹהֵי יַעֲקֹב סֶלָה׃ אֲדֹנָי צְבָאוֹת אַשְׁרֵי אָדָם בֹּטֵחַ בָּךְ׃ אֲדֹנָי הוֹשִׁיעָה הַמֶּלֶךְ יַעֲנֵנוּ בְּיוֹם קָרְאֵנוּ׃ לַיְּהוּדִים הָיְתָה אוֹרָה וְשִׂמְחָה וְשָׂשׁוֹן וִיקָר כֵּן תִּהְיֶה לָנוּ׃ כּוֹס יְשׁוּעוֹת אֶשָּׂא וּבְשֵׁם אֲדֹנָי אֶקְרָא׃ | Behold, God is my salvation; I will trust and not be afraid, for the LORD God is my strength and my song, and he has become my salvation. You will draw water with joy from the springs of salvation. Salvation belongs to the LORD; your blessing is upon your people, Selah. The LORD of hosts is with us; the God of Jacob is our stronghold, Selah. LORD of hosts, happy is the one who trusts in you. LORD, save; may the King answer us on the day we call. The Jews had light and gladness, joy and honour; so may it be for us. I will lift the cup of salvation and call on the name of the LORD. | hineh el yeshu'ati evtach velo efchad ki azi vezimrat yah adonai vayhi li lishu'ah ushe'avtem mayim besashon mima'aynei hayshu'ah la'adonai hayshu'ah al amekha virkhatekha selah adonai tzeva'ot imanu misgav lanu elohei ya'akov selah adonai tzeva'ot ashrei adam boteach bakh adonai hoshi'ah hamelekh ya'anenu beyom kare'enu layehudim hayetah orah vesimchah vesashon vikar ken tihyeh lanu kos yeshu'ot esa uveshem adonai ekra |

| [kaddish-1-l1](f/israeli/line/kaddish-1-l1.mp3) | יִתְגַּדַּל וְיִתְקַדַּשׁ שְׁמֵהּ רַבָּא אָמֵן | Magnified and sanctified be his great name. (Amen.) | yitgadal veyitkadash shemeh raba amen |
| [kaddish-1-l2](f/israeli/line/kaddish-1-l2.mp3) | בְּעָלְמָא דִּי־בְרָא כִרְעוּתֵהּ וְיַמְלִיךְ מַלְכוּתֵהּ בְּחַיֵּיכוֹן וּבְיוֹמֵיכוֹן וּבְחַיֵּי דְכָל־בֵּית יִשְׂרָאֵל בַּעֲגָלָא וּבִזְמַן קָרִיב וְאִמְרוּ אָמֵן׃ | In the world that he created by his will; may he establish his kingdom in your lifetime and in your days and in the lifetime of all the house of Israel, speedily and soon; and say: Amen. | be'alema di-vera khir'uteh veyamlikh malkhuteh bechayeikhon uveyomeikhon uvechayei dekhal-beit yisra'el ba'agala uvizman kariv ve'imru amen |
| [kaddish-1-l3](f/israeli/line/kaddish-1-l3.mp3) | יְהֵא שְׁמֵהּ רַבָּא מְבָרַךְ לְעָלַם וּלְעָלְמֵי עָלְמַיָּא | May his great name be blessed for ever and for all eternities. | yehe shemeh raba mevarakh le'alam ule'alemei alemaya |
| [kaddish-1-l4](f/israeli/line/kaddish-1-l4.mp3) | יִתְבָּרַךְ וְיִשְׁתַּבַּח וְיִתְפָּאַר וְיִתְרוֹמַם וְיִתְנַשֵּׂא וְיִתְהַדָּר וְיִתְעַלֶּה וְיִתְהַלָּל שְׁמֵהּ דְקוּדְשָׁא בְּרִיךְ הוּא | Blessed, praised, glorified, exalted, extolled, honoured, raised and lauded be the name of the Holy One, blessed be he, | yitbarakh veyishtabach veyitpa'ar veyitromam veyitnase veyithadar veyit'aleh veyithalal shemeh dekudesha berikh hu |
| [kaddish-1-l5](f/israeli/line/kaddish-1-l5.mp3) | לְעֵלָּא מִן־כָּל־בִּרְכָתָא וְשִׁירָתָא תֻּשְׁבְּחָתָא וְנֶחָמָתָא דַּאֲמִירָן בְּעָלְמָא וְאִמְרוּ אָמֵן׃ | beyond all the blessings and songs, praises and consolations that are uttered in the world; and say: Amen. | le'ela min-kol-birkhata veshirata tushbechata venechamata da'amiran be'alema ve'imru amen |
| [kaddish-1-l6](f/israeli/line/kaddish-1-l6.mp3) | יְהֵא שְׁלָמָא רַבָּא מִן־שְׁמַיָּא וְחַיִּים עָלֵינוּ וְעַל־כָּל־יִשְׂרָאֵל וְאִמְרוּ אָמֵן׃ | May there be great peace from heaven, and life, for us and for all Israel; and say: Amen. | yehe shelama raba min-shemaya vechayim aleinu ve'al-kol-yisra'el ve'imru amen |
| [kaddish-1-l7](f/israeli/line/kaddish-1-l7.mp3) | עוֹשֶׂה שָׁלוֹם בִּמְרוֹמָיו הוּא יַעֲשֶׂה שָׁלוֹם עָלֵינוּ וְעַל כָּל יִשְׂרָאֵל וְאִמְרוּ אָמֵן׃ | He who makes peace in his high places, may he make peace for us and for all Israel; and say: Amen. | oseh shalom bimromav hu ya'aseh shalom aleinu ve'al kol yisra'el ve'imru amen |
| [kedushah-baruch](f/israeli/line/kedushah-baruch.mp3) | בָּרוּךְ כְּבוֹד־אֲדֹנָי מִמְּקוֹמוֹ׃ | Blessed be the glory of the LORD from his place. | barukh kevod-adonai mimekomo |
| [kedushah-kadosh](f/israeli/line/kedushah-kadosh.mp3) | וְקָרָא זֶה אֶל־זֶה וְאָמַר קָדוֹשׁ קָדוֹשׁ קָדוֹשׁ אֲדֹנָי צְבָאוֹת מְלֹא כָל־הָאָרֶץ כְּבוֹדוֹ׃ | And one called unto another, and said: Holy, holy, holy, is the LORD of hosts; The whole earth is full of His glory. | vekara zeh el-zeh ve'amar kadosh kadosh kadosh adonai tzeva'ot melo khol-ha'aretz kevodo |
| [kedushah-yimloch](f/israeli/line/kedushah-yimloch.mp3) | יִמְלֹךְ אֲדֹנָי לְעוֹלָם אֱלֹהַיִךְ צִיּוֹן לְדֹר וָדֹר הַלְלוּ־יָהּ׃ | The LORD will reign for ever, Thy God, O Zion, unto all generations. Hallelujah. | yimlokh adonai le'olam elohayikh tziyon ledor vador halelu-yah |
| [kiddush-gen-2-1-3-l1](f/israeli/line/kiddush-gen-2-1-3-l1.mp3) | וַיְכֻלּוּ הַשָּׁמַיִם וְהָאָרֶץ וְכָל־צְבָאָם׃ | And the heaven and the earth were finished, and all the host of them. | vaykhulu hashamayim veha'aretz vekhol-tzeva'am |
| [kiddush-gen-2-1-3-l2](f/israeli/line/kiddush-gen-2-1-3-l2.mp3) | וַיְכַל אֱלֹהִים בַּיּוֹם הַשְּׁבִיעִי מְלַאכְתּוֹ אֲשֶׁר עָשָׂה וַיִּשְׁבֹּת בַּיּוֹם הַשְּׁבִיעִי מִכָּל־מְלַאכְתּוֹ אֲשֶׁר עָשָׂה׃ | And on the seventh day God finished His work which He had made; and He rested on the seventh day from all His work which He had made. | vaykhal elohim bayom hashevi'i melakhto asher asah vayishbot bayom hashevi'i mikol-melakhto asher asah |
| [kiddush-gen-2-1-3-l3](f/israeli/line/kiddush-gen-2-1-3-l3.mp3) | וַיְבָרֶךְ אֱלֹהִים אֶת־יוֹם הַשְּׁבִיעִי וַיְקַדֵּשׁ אֹתוֹ כִּי בוֹ שָׁבַת מִכָּל־מְלַאכְתּוֹ אֲשֶׁר־בָּרָא אֱלֹהִים לַעֲשׂוֹת׃ | And God blessed the seventh day, and hallowed it; because that in it He rested from all His work which God in creating had made. | vayvarekh elohim et-yom hashevi'i vaykadesh oto ki vo shavat mikol-melakhto asher-bara elohim la'asot |
| [kiddush-shabbat-eve](f/israeli/line/kiddush-shabbat-eve.mp3) | בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם אֲשֶׁר קִדְּשָׁנוּ בְּמִצְוֹתָיו וְרָצָה בָנוּ וְשַׁבַּת קָדְשׁוֹ בְּאַהֲבָה וּבְרָצוֹן הִנְחִילָנוּ זִכָּרוֹן לְמַעֲשֵׂה בְרֵאשִׁית כִּי הוּא יוֹם תְּחִלָּה לְמִקְרָאֵי קֹדֶשׁ זֵכֶר לִיצִיאַת מִצְרָיִם כִּי בָנוּ בָחַרְתָּ וְאוֹתָנוּ קִדַּשְׁתָּ מִכָּל הָעַמִּים וְשַׁבַּת קָדְשְׁךָ בְּאַהֲבָה וּבְרָצוֹן הִנְחַלְתָּנוּ׃ בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה אֲדֹנָי מְקַדֵּשׁ הַשַּׁבָּת׃ | Blessed are you, LORD our God, king of the world, who made us holy with his commandments and was pleased with us, and in love and favour gave us his holy Shabbat as an inheritance, a remembrance of the work of creation. For it is the first of the holy convocations, a remembrance of the exodus from Egypt. For you chose us and made us holy from all the peoples, and in love and favour you gave us your holy Shabbat as an inheritance. Blessed are you, LORD, who makes the Shabbat holy. | barukh atah adonai eloheinu melekh ha'olam asher kideshanu bemitzvotav veratzah vanu veshabat kodsho be'ahavah uveratzon hinchilanu zikaron lema'aseh vereshit ki hu yom techilah lemikra'ei kodesh zekher litzi'at mitzrayim ki vanu vacharta ve'otanu kidashta mikol ha'amim veshabat kadshekha be'ahavah uveratzon hinchaltanu barukh atah adonai mekadesh hashabat |
| [kiddush-veshamru-l1](f/israeli/line/kiddush-veshamru-l1.mp3) | וְשָׁמְרוּ בְנֵי־יִשְׂרָאֵל אֶת־הַשַּׁבָּת לַעֲשׂוֹת אֶת־הַשַּׁבָּת לְדֹרֹתָם בְּרִית עוֹלָם׃ | Wherefore the children of Israel shall keep the sabbath, to observe the sabbath throughout their generations, for a perpetual covenant. | veshamru venei-yisra'el et-hashabat la'asot et-hashabat ledorotam berit olam |
| [kiddush-veshamru-l2](f/israeli/line/kiddush-veshamru-l2.mp3) | בֵּינִי וּבֵין בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל אוֹת הִוא לְעֹלָם כִּי־שֵׁשֶׁת יָמִים עָשָׂה אֲדֹנָי אֶת־הַשָּׁמַיִם וְאֶת־הָאָרֶץ וּבַיּוֹם הַשְּׁבִיעִי שָׁבַת וַיִּנָּפַשׁ׃ | It is a sign between Me and the children of Israel for ever; for in six days the LORD made heaven and earth, and on the seventh day He ceased from work and rested.’ | beini uvein benei yisra'el ot hiv le'olam ki-sheshet yamim asah adonai et-hashamayim ve'et-ha'aretz uvayom hashevi'i shavat vayinafash |
| [kiddush-wine-1](f/israeli/line/kiddush-wine-1.mp3) | בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם בּוֹרֵא פְּרִי הַגָּפֶן׃ | Blessed are you, LORD our God, king of the world, who creates the fruit of the vine. | barukh atah adonai eloheinu melekh ha'olam bore peri hagafen |
| [kiddush-zachor-l1](f/israeli/line/kiddush-zachor-l1.mp3) | זָכוֹר אֶת־יוֹם הַשַׁבָּת לְקַדְּשׁוֹ | Remember the sabbath day, to keep it holy. | zakhor et-yom hashabat lekadesho |
| [kiddush-zachor-l2](f/israeli/line/kiddush-zachor-l2.mp3) | שֵׁשֶׁת יָמִים תַּעֲבֹד וְעָשִׂיתָ כָּל־מְלַאכְתֶּךָ | Six days shalt thou labour, and do all thy work; | sheshet yamim ta'avod ve'asita kol-melakhtekha |
| [kiddush-zachor-l3](f/israeli/line/kiddush-zachor-l3.mp3) | וְיוֹם הַשְּׁבִיעִי שַׁבָּת לַאֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֶיךָ לֹא־תַעֲשֶׂה כָל־מְלָאכָה אַתָּה וּבִנְךָ־וּבִתֶּךָ עַבְדְּךָ וַאֲמָתְךָ וּבְהֶמְתֶּךָ וְגֵרְךָ אֲשֶׁר בִּשְׁעָרֶיךָ | but the seventh day is a sabbath unto the LORD thy God, in it thou shalt not do any manner of work, thou, nor thy son, nor thy daughter, nor thy man-servant, nor thy maid-servant, nor thy cattle, nor thy stranger that is within thy gates; | veyom hashevi'i shabat la'adonai elohekha lo-ta'aseh khol-melakhah atah uvinkha-uvitekha avdekha va'amatekha uvehemtekha vegerekha asher bish'arekha |
| [kiddush-zachor-l4](f/israeli/line/kiddush-zachor-l4.mp3) | כִּי שֵׁשֶׁת־יָמִים עָשָׂה אֲדֹנָי אֶת־הַשָּׁמַיִם וְאֶת־הָאָרֶץ אֶת־הַיָּם וְאֶת־כָּל־אֲשֶׁר־בָּם וַיָּנַח בַּיּוֹם הַשְּׁבִיעִי עַל־כֵּן בֵּרַךְ אֲדֹנָי אֶת־יוֹם הַשַׁבָּת וַיְקַדְּשֵׁהוּ׃ | for in six days the LORD made heaven and earth, the sea, and all that in them is, and rested on the seventh day; wherefore the LORD blessed the sabbath day, and hallowed it. | ki sheshet-yamim asah adonai et-hashamayim ve'et-ha'aretz et-hayam ve'et-kol-asher-bam vayanach bayom hashevi'i al-ken berakh adonai et-yom hashabat vaykadeshehu |
| [lecha-dodi-1-l1](f/israeli/line/lecha-dodi-1-l1.mp3) | לְכָה דוֹדִי לִקְרַאת כַּלָּה פְּנֵי שַׁבָּת נְקַבְּלָה׃ | Come, my beloved, to meet the bride; let us welcome the presence of Shabbat. | lekhah dodi likrat kalah penei shabat nekabelah |
| [lecha-dodi-1-l2](f/israeli/line/lecha-dodi-1-l2.mp3) | שָׁמוֹר וְזָכוֹר בְּדִבּוּר אֶחָד הִשְׁמִיעָנוּ אֵל הַמְיוּחָד אֲדֹנָי אֶחָד וּשְׁמוֹ אֶחָד לְשֵׁם וּלְתִפְאֶרֶת וְלִתְהִלָּה׃ | "Keep" and "remember" in a single utterance, the one God made us hear; the LORD is one and his name is one, for renown, for glory and for praise. | shamor vezakhor bedibur echad hishmi'anu el hamyuchad adonai echad ushemo echad leshem uletif'eret velithilah |
| [lecha-hashem](f/israeli/line/lecha-hashem.mp3) | לְךָ אֲדֹנָי הַגְּדֻלָּה וְהַגְּבוּרָה וְהַתִּפְאֶרֶת וְהַנֵּצַח וְהַהוֹד כִּי כֹל בַּשָּׁמַיִם וּבָאָרֶץ׃ לְךָ אֲדֹנָי הַמַּמְלָכָה וְהַמִּתְנַשֵּׂא לְכֹל לְרֹאשׁ׃ רוֹמְמוּ אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵינוּ וְהִשְׁתַּחֲווּ לַהֲדֹם רַגְלָיו קָדוֹשׁ הוּא׃ רוֹמְמוּ אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵינוּ וְהִשְׁתַּחֲווּ לְהַר קָדְשׁוֹ כִּי קָדוֹשׁ אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵינוּ׃ | Yours, LORD, are the greatness and the might and the splendour and the victory and the majesty, for everything in heaven and on earth is yours. Yours, LORD, is the kingdom, and you are exalted as head over all. Exalt the LORD our God and bow at his footstool: he is holy. | [mah-tovu-1](f/israeli/line/mah-tovu-1.mp3) | מַה־טֹּבוּ אֹהָלֶיךָ יַעֲקֹב מִשְׁכְּנֹתֶיךָ יִשְׂרָאֵל׃ | How goodly are thy tents, O Jacob, Thy dwellings, O Israel! | mah-tovu ohalekha ya'akov mishkenotekha yisra'el |
| [malchutcha](f/israeli/line/malchutcha.mp3) | מַלְכוּתְךָ מַלְכוּת כָּל־עֹלָמִים וּמֶמְשֶׁלְתְּךָ בְּכָל־דּוֹר וָדוֹר׃ | Thy kingdom is a kingdom for all ages, And Thy dominion endureth throughout all generations. | malkhutekha malkhut kol-olamim umemsheltekha bekhol-dor vador |

| [shema-2](f/israeli/line/shema-2.mp3) | בָּרוּךְ שֵׁם כְּבוֹד מַלְכוּתוֹ לְעוֹלָם וָעֶד׃ | Blessed is the name of the glory of his kingship for ever and ever. | barukh shem kevod malkhuto le'olam va'ed |
| [shema-vahavta-l1](f/israeli/line/shema-vahavta-l1.mp3) | שְׁמַע יִשְׂרָאֵל אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵינוּ אֲדֹנָי אֶחָד׃ | HEAR, O ISRAEL: THE LORD OUR GOD, THE LORD IS ONE. | shema yisra'el adonai eloheinu adonai echad |
| [shema-vahavta-l2](f/israeli/line/shema-vahavta-l2.mp3) | וְאָהַבְתָּ אֵת אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֶיךָ בְּכָל־לְבָבְךָ וּבְכָל־נַפְשְׁךָ וּבְכָל־מְאֹדֶךָ׃ | And thou shalt love the LORD thy God with all thy heart, and with all thy soul, and with all thy might. | ve'ahavta et adonai elohekha bekhol-levavekha uvekhol-nafshekha uvekhol-me'odekha |
| [shema-vahavta-l3](f/israeli/line/shema-vahavta-l3.mp3) | וְהָיוּ הַדְּבָרִים הָאֵלֶּה אֲשֶׁר אָנֹכִי מְצַוְּךָ הַיּוֹם עַל־לְבָבֶךָ׃ | And these words, which I command thee this day, shall be upon thy heart; | vehayu hadevarim ha'eleh asher anokhi metzavekha hayom al-levavekha |
| [shema-vahavta-l4](f/israeli/line/shema-vahavta-l4.mp3) | וְשִׁנַּנְתָּם לְבָנֶיךָ וְדִבַּרְתָּ בָּם בְּשִׁבְתְּךָ בְּבֵיתֶךָ וּבְלֶכְתְּךָ בַדֶּרֶךְ וּבְשָׁכְבְּךָ וּבְקוּמֶךָ׃ | and thou shalt teach them diligently unto thy children, and shalt talk of them when thou sittest in thy house, and when thou walkest by the way, and when thou liest down, and when thou risest up. | veshinantam levanekha vedibarta bam beshivtekha beveitekha uvelekhtekha vaderekh uveshakhbekha uvekumekha |
| [shema-vahavta-l5](f/israeli/line/shema-vahavta-l5.mp3) | וּקְשַׁרְתָּם לְאוֹת עַל־יָדֶךָ וְהָיוּ לְטֹטָפֹת בֵּין עֵינֶיךָ׃ | And thou shalt bind them for a sign upon thy hand, and they shall be for frontlets between thine eyes. | ukeshartam le'ot al-yadekha vehayu letotafot bein einekha |
| [shema-vahavta-l6](f/israeli/line/shema-vahavta-l6.mp3) | וּכְתַבְתָּם עַל־מְזוּזֹת בֵּיתֶךָ וּבִשְׁעָרֶיךָ׃ | And thou shalt write them upon the door-posts of thy house, and upon thy gates. | ukhetavtam al-mezuzot beitekha uvish'arekha |
| [shir-hamaalot-l1](f/israeli/line/shir-hamaalot-l1.mp3) | שִׁיר הַמַּעֲלוֹת בְּשׁוּב אֲדֹנָי אֶת־שִׁיבַת צִיּוֹן הָיִינוּ כְּחֹלְמִים׃ | A Song of Ascents. When the LORD brought back those that returned to Zion, We were like unto them that dream. | shir hama'alot beshuv adonai et-shivat tziyon hayinu kecholemim |
| [shir-hamaalot-l2](f/israeli/line/shir-hamaalot-l2.mp3) | אָז יִמָּלֵא שְׂחוֹק פִּינוּ וּלְשׁוֹנֵנוּ רִנָּה אָז יֹאמְרוּ בַגּוֹיִם הִגְדִּיל אֲדֹנָי לַעֲשׂוֹת עִם־אֵלֶּה׃ | Then was our mouth filled with laughter, and our tongue with singing; then said they among the nations: 'The LORD hath done great things with these.' | az yimale sechok pinu uleshonenu rinah az yomru vagoyim higdil adonai la'asot im-eleh |
| [shir-hamaalot-l3](f/israeli/line/shir-hamaalot-l3.mp3) | הִגְדִּיל אֲדֹנָי לַעֲשׂוֹת עִמָּנוּ הָיִינוּ שְׂמֵחִים׃ | The LORD hath done great things with us; we are rejoiced. | higdil adonai la'asot imanu hayinu semechim |

| [sim-shalom-l1](f/israeli/line/sim-shalom-l1.mp3) | שִׂים שָׁלוֹם טוֹבָה וּבְרָכָה חֵן וָחֶסֶד וְרַחֲמִים עָלֵינוּ וְעַל כָּל־יִשְׂרָאֵל עַמֶּךָ בָּרְכֵנוּ אָבִינוּ כֻּלָּנוּ כְּאֶחָד בְּאוֹר פָּנֶיךָ כִּי בְאוֹר פָּנֶיךָ נָתַתָּ לָּנוּ אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵינוּ תּוֹרַת חַיִּים וְאַהֲבַת חֶסֶד וּצְדָקָה וּבְרָכָה וְרַחֲמִים וְחַיִּים וְשָׁלוֹם וְטוֹב בְּעֵינֶיךָ לְבָרֵךְ אֶת עַמְּךָ יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּכָל־עֵת וּבְכָל־שָׁעָה בִּשְׁלוֹמֶךָ׃ | Grant peace, goodness and blessing, grace, kindness and mercy upon us and upon all Israel your people. Bless us, our father, all of us as one, with the light of your face; for by the light of your face you gave us, LORD our God, a Torah of life, love of kindness, righteousness, blessing, mercy, life and peace. And may it be good in your eyes to bless your people Israel at every time and every hour with your peace. | sim shalom tovah uverakhah chen vachesed verachamim aleinu ve'al kol-yisra'el amekha barekhenu avinu kulanu ke'echad be'or panekha ki ve'or panekha natata lanu adonai eloheinu torat chayim ve'ahavat chesed utzedakah uverakhah verachamim vechayim veshalom vetov be'einekha levarekh et amekha yisra'el bekhol-et uvekhol-sha'ah bishlomekha |

| [torah-bless-1](f/israeli/line/torah-bless-1.mp3) | בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם אֲשֶׁר בָּחַר בָּנוּ מִכָּל־הָעַמִּים וְנָתַן לָנוּ אֶת־תּוֹרָתוֹ בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה אֲדֹנָי נוֹתֵן הַתּוֹרָה׃ | Blessed are you, LORD our God, king of the world, who chose us from all the peoples and gave us his Torah. Blessed are you, LORD, giver of the Torah. | barukh atah adonai eloheinu melekh ha'olam asher bachar banu mikol-ha'amim venatan lanu et-torato barukh atah adonai noten hatorah |
| [torah-bless-2](f/israeli/line/torah-bless-2.mp3) | בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם אֲשֶׁר נָתַן לָנוּ תּוֹרַת אֱמֶת וְחַיֵּי עוֹלָם נָטַע בְּתוֹכֵנוּ׃ בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה אֲדֹנָי נוֹתֵן הַתּוֹרָה׃ | Blessed are you, LORD our God, king of the world, who gave us a Torah of truth and planted eternal life in our midst. Blessed are you, LORD, giver of the Torah. | barukh atah adonai eloheinu melekh ha'olam asher natan lanu torat emet vechayei olam nata betokhenu barukh atah adonai noten hatorah |
| [vahavta-1](f/israeli/line/vahavta-1.mp3) | וְאָהַבְתָּ אֵת אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֶיךָ בְּכָל־לְבָבְךָ וּבְכָל־נַפְשְׁךָ וּבְכָל־מְאֹדֶךָ׃ | And thou shalt love the LORD thy God with all thy heart, and with all thy soul, and with all thy might. | ve'ahavta et adonai elohekha bekhol-levavekha uvekhol-nafshekha uvekhol-me'odekha |
| [vahavta-2-l1](f/israeli/line/vahavta-2-l1.mp3) | וְהָיוּ הַדְּבָרִים הָאֵלֶּה אֲשֶׁר אָנֹכִי מְצַוְּךָ הַיּוֹם עַל־לְבָבֶךָ׃ | And these words, which I command thee this day, shall be upon thy heart; | vehayu hadevarim ha'eleh asher anokhi metzavekha hayom al-levavekha |
| [vahavta-2-l2](f/israeli/line/vahavta-2-l2.mp3) | וְשִׁנַּנְתָּם לְבָנֶיךָ וְדִבַּרְתָּ בָּם בְּשִׁבְתְּךָ בְּבֵיתֶךָ וּבְלֶכְתְּךָ בַדֶּרֶךְ וּבְשָׁכְבְּךָ וּבְקוּמֶךָ׃ | and thou shalt teach them diligently unto thy children, and shalt talk of them when thou sittest in thy house, and when thou walkest by the way, and when thou liest down, and when thou risest up. | veshinantam levanekha vedibarta bam beshivtekha beveitekha uvelekhtekha vaderekh uveshakhbekha uvekumekha |
| [vahavta-full-l1](f/israeli/line/vahavta-full-l1.mp3) | וְאָהַבְתָּ אֵת אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֶיךָ בְּכָל־לְבָבְךָ וּבְכָל־נַפְשְׁךָ וּבְכָל־מְאֹדֶךָ׃ | And thou shalt love the LORD thy God with all thy heart, and with all thy soul, and with all thy might. | ve'ahavta et adonai elohekha bekhol-levavekha uvekhol-nafshekha uvekhol-me'odekha |
| [vahavta-full-l2](f/israeli/line/vahavta-full-l2.mp3) | וְהָיוּ הַדְּבָרִים הָאֵלֶּה אֲשֶׁר אָנֹכִי מְצַוְּךָ הַיּוֹם עַל־לְבָבֶךָ׃ | And these words, which I command thee this day, shall be upon thy heart; | vehayu hadevarim ha'eleh asher anokhi metzavekha hayom al-levavekha |
| [vahavta-full-l3](f/israeli/line/vahavta-full-l3.mp3) | וְשִׁנַּנְתָּם לְבָנֶיךָ וְדִבַּרְתָּ בָּם בְּשִׁבְתְּךָ בְּבֵיתֶךָ וּבְלֶכְתְּךָ בַדֶּרֶךְ וּבְשָׁכְבְּךָ וּבְקוּמֶךָ׃ | and thou shalt teach them diligently unto thy children, and shalt talk of them when thou sittest in thy house, and when thou walkest by the way, and when thou liest down, and when thou risest up. | veshinantam levanekha vedibarta bam beshivtekha beveitekha uvelekhtekha vaderekh uveshakhbekha uvekumekha |
| [vahavta-full-l4](f/israeli/line/vahavta-full-l4.mp3) | וּקְשַׁרְתָּם לְאוֹת עַל־יָדֶךָ וְהָיוּ לְטֹטָפֹת בֵּין עֵינֶיךָ׃ | And thou shalt bind them for a sign upon thy hand, and they shall be for frontlets between thine eyes. | ukeshartam le'ot al-yadekha vehayu letotafot bein einekha |
| [vahavta-full-l5](f/israeli/line/vahavta-full-l5.mp3) | וּכְתַבְתָּם עַל־מְזוּזֹת בֵּיתֶךָ וּבִשְׁעָרֶיךָ׃ | And thou shalt write them upon the door-posts of thy house, and upon thy gates. | ukhetavtam al-mezuzot beitekha uvish'arekha |
| [vayehi-binsoa](f/israeli/line/vayehi-binsoa.mp3) | וַיְהִי בִּנְסֹעַ הָאָרֹן וַיֹאמֶר מֹשֶׁה קוּמָה אֲדֹנָי וְיָפֻצוּ אֹיְבֶיךָ וְיָנֻסוּ מְשַׂנְאֶיךָ מִפָּנֶיךָ׃ כִּי מִצִּיּוֹן תֵּצֵא תוֹרָה וּדְבַר אֲדֹנָי מִירוּשָׁלָםִ׃ בָּרוּךְ שֶׁנָּתַן תּוֹרָה לְעַמּוֹ יִשְׂרָאֵל בִּקְדֻשָּׁתוֹ׃ | And it was, when the ark set out, Moses said: Arise, LORD, and let your enemies be scattered, and let those who hate you flee before you. For from Zion will go forth Torah, and the word of the LORD from Jerusalem. Blessed is the one who gave the Torah to his people Israel in his holiness. | vayhi binso'a ha'aron vayomer mosheh kumah adonai veyafutzu oyevekha veyanusu mesan'ekha mipanekha ki mitziyon tetze torah udevar adonai mirushalami barukh shenatan torah le'amo yisra'el bikdushato |
| [vezot-hatorah](f/israeli/line/vezot-hatorah.mp3) | וְזֹאת הַתּוֹרָה אֲשֶׁר שָׂם מֹשֶׁה לִפְנֵי בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל׃ עַל פִּי אֲדֹנָי בְּיַד מֹשֶׁה׃ | This is the Torah that Moses set before the children of Israel, by the word of the LORD, by the hand of Moses. | vezot hatorah asher sam mosheh lifnei benei yisra'el al pi adonai beyad mosheh |
| [yehalelu-l1](f/israeli/line/yehalelu-l1.mp3) | יְהַלְלוּ אֶת־שֵׁם אֲדֹנָי כִּי־נִשְׂגָּב שְׁמוֹ לְבַדּוֹ הוֹדוֹ עַל־אֶרֶץ וְשָׁמָיִם׃ | Let them praise the name of the LORD, For His name alone is exalted; His glory is above the earth and heaven. | yehalelu et-shem adonai ki-nisgav shemo levado hodo al-eretz veshamayim |
| [yehalelu-l2](f/israeli/line/yehalelu-l2.mp3) | וַיָּרֶם קֶרֶן לְעַמּוֹ תְּהִלָּה לְכָל־חֲסִידָיו לִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל עַם־קְרֹבוֹ הַלְלוּ־יָהּ׃ | And He hath lifted up a horn for His people, A praise for all His saints, Even for the children of Israel, a people near unto Him. Hallelujah. | vayarem keren le'amo tehilah lekhol-chasidav livnei yisra'el am-kerovo halelu-yah |
| [yigdal-l1](f/israeli/line/yigdal-l1.mp3) | יִגְדַּל אֱלֹהִים חַי וְיִשְׁתַּבַּח נִמְצָא וְאֵין עֵת אֶל מְצִיאוּתוֹ׃ | May the living God be magnified and praised; he exists, and his existence is not bound by time. | yigdal elohim chai veyishtabach nimtza ve'ein et el metzi'uto |
| [yigdal-l10](f/israeli/line/yigdal-l10.mp3) | צוֹפֶה וְיוֹדֵעַ סְתָרֵינוּ מַבִּיט לְסוֹף דָּבָר בְּקַדְמָתוֹ׃ | He watches and knows our secrets; he sees the end of a matter at its beginning. | tzofeh veyode'a setareinu mabit lesof davar bekadmato |
| [yigdal-l11](f/israeli/line/yigdal-l11.mp3) | גּוֹמֵל לְאִישׁ חֶסֶד כְּמִפְעָלוֹ נוֹתֵן לְרָשָׁע רָע כְּרִשְׁעָתוֹ׃ | He repays a person with kindness according to his deeds; he gives the wicked evil according to his wickedness. | gomel le'ish chesed kemif'alo noten lerasha ra kerish'ato |
| [yigdal-l12](f/israeli/line/yigdal-l12.mp3) | יִשְׁלַח לְקֵץ הַיָּמִין מְשִׁיחֵנוּ לִפְדּוֹת מְחַכֵּי קֵץ יְשׁוּעָתוֹ׃ | At the end of days he will send our anointed one, to redeem those who await the end of his salvation. | yishlach leketz hayamin meshichenu lifdot mechakei ketz yeshu'ato |
| [yigdal-l13](f/israeli/line/yigdal-l13.mp3) | מֵתִים יְחַיֶּה אֵל בְּרֹב חַסְדּוֹ בָּרוּךְ עֲדֵי עַד שֵׁם תְּהִלָּתוֹ׃ | God will revive the dead in his great kindness; blessed for ever is his praised name. | metim yechayeh el berov chasdo barukh adei ad shem tehilato |
| [yigdal-l2](f/israeli/line/yigdal-l2.mp3) | אֶחָד וְאֵין יָחִיד כְּיִחוּדוֹ נֶעְלָם וְגַם אֵין סוֹף לְאַחְדּוּתוֹ׃ | He is one, and there is no unity like his unity; hidden, and without end to his oneness. | echad ve'ein yachid keyichudo nelam vegam ein sof le'achduto |
| [yigdal-l3](f/israeli/line/yigdal-l3.mp3) | אֵין לוֹ דְמוּת הַגּוּף וְאֵינוֹ גוּף לֹא נַעֲרֹךְ אֵלָיו קְדֻשָּׁתוֹ׃ | He has no bodily form and is not a body; his holiness cannot be compared. | ein lo demut haguf ve'eino guf lo na'arokh elav kedushato |
| [yigdal-l4](f/israeli/line/yigdal-l4.mp3) | קַדְמוֹן לְכָל־דָּבָר אֲשֶׁר נִבְרָא רִאשׁוֹן וְאֵין רֵאשִׁית לְרֵאשִׁיתוֹ׃ | He preceded everything that was created; first, and with no beginning to his beginning. | kadmon lekhol-davar asher nivra rishon ve'ein reshit lereshito |
| [yigdal-l5](f/israeli/line/yigdal-l5.mp3) | הִנּוֹ אֲדוֹן עוֹלָם לְכָל־נוֹצָר יוֹרֶה גְדֻלָּתוֹ וּמַלְכוּתוֹ׃ | Behold, he is master of the world to every creature; he shows his greatness and his sovereignty. | hino adon olam lekhol-notzar yoreh gedulato umalkhuto |
| [yigdal-l6](f/israeli/line/yigdal-l6.mp3) | שֶׁפַע נְבוּאָתוֹ נְתָנוֹ אֶל אַנְשֵׁי סְגֻלָּתוֹ וְתִפְאַרְתּוֹ׃ | The abundance of his prophecy he gave to the people he treasured and who are his glory. | shefa nevu'ato netano el anshei segulato vetif'arto |
| [yigdal-l7](f/israeli/line/yigdal-l7.mp3) | לֹא קָם בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל כְּמֹשֶׁה עוֹד נָבִיא וּמַבִּיט אֶת־תְּמוּנָתוֹ׃ | No one has arisen in Israel like Moses, a prophet who beheld his likeness. | lo kam beyisra'el kemosheh od navi umabit et-temunato |
| [yigdal-l8](f/israeli/line/yigdal-l8.mp3) | תּוֹרַת אֱמֶת נָתַן לְעַמּוֹ אֵל עַל יַד נְבִיאוֹ נֶאֱמַן בֵּיתוֹ׃ | A Torah of truth God gave to his people through his prophet, trusted in his house. | torat emet natan le'amo el al yad nevi'o ne'eman beito |
| [yigdal-l9](f/israeli/line/yigdal-l9.mp3) | לֹא יַחֲלִיף הָאֵל וְלֹא יָמִיר דָּתוֹ לְעוֹלָמִים לְזוּלָתוֹ׃ | God will not alter or exchange his law, for ever, for any other. | lo yachalif ha'el velo yamir dato le'olamim lezulato |
| [zimun-opening-l1](f/israeli/line/zimun-opening-l1.mp3) | רַבּוֹתַי נְבָרֵךְ׃ | Gentlemen, let us bless. | rabotai nevarekh |
| [zimun-opening-l2](f/israeli/line/zimun-opening-l2.mp3) | יְהִי שֵׁם אֲדֹנָי מְבֹרָךְ מֵעַתָּה וְעַד־עוֹלָם׃ | May the name of the LORD be blessed from now and for ever. | yehi shem adonai mevorakh me'atah ve'ad-olam |
| [adon-olam-1-l1](m/israeli/line/adon-olam-1-l1.mp3) | אֲדוֹן עוֹלָם אֲשֶׁר מָלַךְ בְּטֶרֶם כָּל יְצִיר נִבְרָא׃ | Lord of the world, who reigned before any form was created. | adon olam asher malakh beterem kol yetzir nivra |
| [adon-olam-1-l10](m/israeli/line/adon-olam-1-l10.mp3) | וְעִם רוּחִי גְּוִיָּתִי אֲדֹנָי לִי וְלֹא אִירָא׃ | And with my spirit, my body too: the LORD is with me and I will not fear. | ve'im ruchi geviyati adonai li velo ira |
| [adon-olam-1-l2](m/israeli/line/adon-olam-1-l2.mp3) | לְעֵת נַעֲשָׂה בְחֶפְצוֹ כֹּל אֲזַי מֶלֶךְ שְׁמוֹ נִקְרָא׃ | At the time when all was made by his will, then "King" was his name proclaimed. | le'et na'asah vecheftzo kol azai melekh shemo nikra |
| [adon-olam-1-l3](m/israeli/line/adon-olam-1-l3.mp3) | וְאַחֲרֵי כִּכְלוֹת הַכֹּל לְבַדּוֹ יִמְלֹךְ נוֹרָא׃ | And after all things have ended, he alone, the awesome one, will reign. | ve'acharei kikhlot hakol levado yimlokh nora |
| [adon-olam-1-l4](m/israeli/line/adon-olam-1-l4.mp3) | וְהוּא הָיָה וְהוּא הֹוֶה וְהוּא יִהְיֶה בְּתִפְאָרָה׃ | And he was, and he is, and he will be, in glory. | vehu hayah vehu hoveh vehu yihyeh betif'arah |
| [adon-olam-1-l5](m/israeli/line/adon-olam-1-l5.mp3) | וְהוּא אֶחָד וְאֵין שֵׁנִי לְהַמְשִׁיל לוֹ לְהַחְבִּירָה׃ | And he is one, and there is no second, to compare to him or to join with him. | vehu echad ve'ein sheni lehamshil lo lehachbirah |
| [adon-olam-1-l6](m/israeli/line/adon-olam-1-l6.mp3) | בְּלִי רֵאשִׁית בְּלִי תַכְלִית וְלוֹ הָעֹז וְהַמִּשְׂרָה׃ | Without beginning, without end; to him belong the power and the dominion. | beli reshit beli takhlit velo ha'oz vehamisrah |
| [adon-olam-1-l7](m/israeli/line/adon-olam-1-l7.mp3) | וְהוּא אֵלִי וְחַי גוֹאֲלִי וְצוּר חֶבְלִי בְּעֵת צָרָה׃ | And he is my God, and my living redeemer, and the rock of my portion in time of distress. | vehu eli vechai go'ali vetzur chevli be'et tzarah |
| [adon-olam-1-l8](m/israeli/line/adon-olam-1-l8.mp3) | וְהוּא נִסִּי וּמָנוֹס לִי מְנָת כּוֹסִי בְּיוֹם אֶקְרָא׃ | And he is my banner and my refuge, the portion of my cup on the day I call. | vehu nisi umanos li menat kosi beyom ekra |
| [adon-olam-1-l9](m/israeli/line/adon-olam-1-l9.mp3) | בְּיָדוֹ אַפְקִיד רוּחִי בְּעֵת אִישָׁן וְאָעִירָה׃ | Into his hand I commit my spirit, when I sleep and when I wake. | beyado afkid ruchi be'et ishan ve'a'irah |
| [adonai-oz](m/israeli/line/adonai-oz.mp3) | אֲדֹנָי עֹז לְעַמּוֹ יִתֵּן אֲדֹנָי יְבָרֵךְ אֶת־עַמּוֹ בַשָּׁלוֹם׃ | The LORD will give strength unto His people; The LORD will bless his people with peace. | adonai oz le'amo yiten adonai yevarekh et-amo vashalom |
| [aleinu-1](m/israeli/line/aleinu-1.mp3) | עָלֵינוּ לְשַׁבֵּחַ לַאֲדוֹן הַכֹּל לָתֵת גְּדֻלָּה לְיוֹצֵר בְּרֵאשִׁית שֶׁלֹּא עָשָׂנוּ כְּגוֹיֵי הָאֲרָצוֹת וְלֹא שָׂמָנוּ כְּמִשְׁפְּחוֹת הָאֲדָמָה שֶׁלֹּא שָׂם חֶלְקֵנוּ כָּהֶם וְגוֹרָלֵנוּ כְּכָל־הֲמוֹנָם שֶׁהֵם מִשְׁתַּחֲוִים לָהֶבֶל וָרִיק וּמִתְפַּלְּלִים אֶל־אֵל לֹא יוֹשִׁיעַ וַאֲנַחְנוּ מִשְׁתַּחֲוִים לִפְנֵי מֶלֶךְ מַלְכֵי הַמְּלָכִים הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא שֶׁהוּא נוֹטֶה שָׁמַיִם וְיוֹסֵד אָרֶץ וּמוֹשַׁב יְקָרוֹ בַּשָּׁמַיִם מִמַּעַל וּשְׁכִינַת עֻזּוֹ בְּגָבְהֵי מְרוֹמִים הוּא אֱלֹהֵינוּ וְאֵין עוֹד אַחֵר אֱמֶת מַלְכֵּנוּ וְאֶפֶס זוּלָתוֹ כַּכָּתוּב בַּתּוֹרָה וְיָדַעְתָּ הַיּוֹם וַהֲשֵׁבֹתָ אֶל־לְבָבֶךָ כִּי אֲדֹנָי הוּא הָאֱלֹהִים בַּשָּׁמַיִם מִמַּעַל וְעַל־הָאָרֶץ מִתָּחַת אֵין עוֹד׃ | It is our duty to praise the Master of all, to ascribe greatness to the Creator of the beginning: that he has not made us like the nations of the lands and has not placed us like the families of the earth, that he has not set our portion like theirs nor our lot like all their multitude. For they bow to vanity and emptiness and pray to a god who does not save; but we bow before the King of kings of kings, the Holy One, blessed be he, who stretches out the heavens and founds the earth, whose seat of glory is in the heavens above and the presence of his might in the loftiest heights. He is our God; there is no other. Truly he is our king; there is nothing besides him. May your good will be with your people Israel for ever; show your people the redemption of your right hand in your Temple, bring us of the goodness of your light, and accept our prayer in mercy. May it be your will to lengthen our life in goodness, and let me be counted among the righteous, so that you have mercy on me and guard me and all that is mine and your people Israel's. | [haftarah-after-l1](m/israeli/line/haftarah-after-l1.mp3) | בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם צוּר כָּל־הָעוֹלָמִים צַדִּיק בְּכָל־הַדּוֹרוֹת הָאֵל הַנֶּאֱמָן הָאֹמֵר וְעֹשֶׂה הַמְדַבֵּר וּמְקַיֵּם שֶׁכָּל־דְּבָרָיו אֱמֶת וָצֶדֶק נֶאֱמָן אַתָּה הוּא אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵינוּ וְנֶאֱמָנִים דְּבָרֶיךָ וְדָבָר אֶחָד מִדְּבָרֶיךָ אָחוֹר לֹא יָשׁוּב רֵיקָם כִּי אֵל מֶלֶךְ נֶאֱמָן וְרַחֲמָן אָתָּה בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה אֲדֹנָי הָאֵל הַנֶּאֱמָן בְּכָל־דְּבָרָיו׃ | Blessed are you, LORD our God, king of the world, rock of all the worlds, righteous in all generations, the faithful God who says and does, who speaks and fulfils, all of whose words are true and just. You are faithful, LORD our God, and your words are faithful, and not one of your words returns empty, for you are a faithful and merciful God and king. Blessed are you, LORD, the God who is faithful in all his words. | barukh atah adonai eloheinu melekh ha'olam tzur kol-ha'olamim tzadik bekhol-hadorot ha'el hane'eman ha'omer ve'oseh hamdaber umekayem shekol-devarav emet vatzedek ne'eman atah hu adonai eloheinu vene'emanim devarekha vedavar echad midevarekha achor lo yashuv reikam ki el melekh ne'eman verachaman atah barukh atah adonai ha'el hane'eman bekhol-devarav |
| [haftarah-after-l2](m/israeli/line/haftarah-after-l2.mp3) | רַחֵם עַל צִיּוֹן כִּי הִיא בֵּית חַיֵּינוּ וְלַעֲלוּבַת נֶפֶשׁ תּוֹשִׁיעַ בִּמְהֵרָה בְיָמֵינוּ בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה אֲדֹנָי מְשַׂמֵּחַ צִיּוֹן בְּבָנֶיהָ׃ | Have mercy on Zion, for it is the home of our life, and save the one with a grieving soul quickly in our days. Blessed are you, LORD, who gladdens Zion through her children. | rachem al tziyon ki hi beit chayeinu vela'aluvat nefesh toshi'a bimherah veyameinu barukh atah adonai mesameach tziyon bevaneha |
| [haftarah-after-l3](m/israeli/line/haftarah-after-l3.mp3) | שַׂמְּחֵנוּ אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵינוּ בְּאֵלִיָהוּ הַנָּבִיא עַבְדָּךְ וּבְמַלְכוּת בֵּית דָּוִד מְשִׁיחָךְ בִּמְהֵרָה יָבֹא וְיָגֵל לִבֵּנוּ עַל כִּסְאוֹ לֹא יֵשֵׁב זָר וְלֹא יִנְחֲלוּ עוֹד אֲחֵרִים אֶת כְּבוֹדוֹ כִּי בְשֵׁם קָדְשְׁךָ נִשְׁבַּעְתָּ לּוֹ שֶׁלֹּא יִכְבֶּה נֵרוֹ לְעוֹלָם וָעֶד בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה אֲדֹנָי מָגֵן דָּוִד׃ | Gladden us, LORD our God, with Elijah the prophet your servant and with the kingdom of the house of David your anointed; may he come quickly and make our heart rejoice. On his throne let no stranger sit, and let others not inherit his glory any more, for by your holy name you swore to him that his light would never go out. Blessed are you, LORD, shield of David. | samechenu adonai eloheinu be'eliyahu hanavi avdakh uvemalkhut beit david meshichakh bimherah yavo veyagel libenu al kis'o lo yeshev zar velo yinchalu od acherim et kevodo ki veshem kadshekha nishbata lo shelo yikhbeh nero le'olam va'ed barukh atah adonai magen david |
| [haftarah-after-l4](m/israeli/line/haftarah-after-l4.mp3) | עַל הַתּוֹרָה וְעַל הָעֲבוֹדָה וְעַל הַנְּבִיאִים וְעַל יוֹם הַשַּׁבָּת הַזֶּה שֶׁנָּתַתָּ לָּנוּ אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵינוּ לִקְדֻשָּׁה וְלִמְנוּחָה לְכָבוֹד וּלְתִפְאָרֶת עַל הַכֹּל אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵינוּ אֲנַחְנוּ מוֹדִים לָךְ וּמְבָרְכִים אוֹתָךְ יִתְבָּרַךְ שִׁמְךָ בְּפִי כָּל־חַי תָּמִיד לְעוֹלָם וָעֶד בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה אֲדֹנָי מְקַדֵּשׁ הַשַּׁבָּת אָמֵן׃ | For the Torah, and for the service, and for the prophets, and for this Shabbat day which you gave us, LORD our God, for holiness and rest, for honour and glory. For all this, LORD our God, we thank you and bless you. May your name be blessed by every living being, always, for ever. | [haftarah-before](m/israeli/line/haftarah-before.mp3) | בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵינוּ מֶלֶךְ הָעוֹלָם אֲשֶׁר בָּחַר בִּנְבִיאִים טוֹבִים וְרָצָה בְדִבְרֵיהֶם הַנֶּאֱמָרִים בֶּאֱמֶת בָּרוּךְ אַתָּה אֲדֹנָי הַבּוֹחֵר בַּתּוֹרָה וּבְמֹשֶׁה עַבְדּוֹ וּבְיִשְׂרָאֵל עַמּוֹ וּבִנְבִיאֵי הָאֱמֶת וְהַצֶּדֶק׃ | Blessed are you, LORD our God, king of the world, who chose good prophets and was pleased with their words, which were spoken in truth. | [shema-vahavta-l1](m/israeli/line/shema-vahavta-l1.mp3) | שְׁמַע יִשְׂרָאֵל אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֵינוּ אֲדֹנָי אֶחָד׃ | HEAR, O ISRAEL: THE LORD OUR GOD, THE LORD IS ONE. | shema yisra'el adonai eloheinu adonai echad |
| [shema-vahavta-l2](m/israeli/line/shema-vahavta-l2.mp3) | וְאָהַבְתָּ אֵת אֲדֹנָי אֱלֹהֶיךָ בְּכָל־לְבָבְךָ וּבְכָל־נַפְשְׁךָ וּבְכָל־מְאֹדֶךָ׃ | And thou shalt love the LORD thy God with all thy heart, and with all thy soul, and with all thy might. | ve'ahavta et adonai elohekha bekhol-levavekha uvekhol-nafshekha uvekhol-me'odekha |
| [shema-vahavta-l3](m/israeli/line/shema-vahavta-l3.mp3) | וְהָיוּ הַדְּבָרִים הָאֵלֶּה אֲשֶׁר אָנֹכִי מְצַוְּךָ הַיּוֹם עַל־לְבָבֶךָ׃ | And these words, which I command thee this day, shall be upon thy heart; | vehayu hadevarim ha'eleh asher anokhi metzavekha hayom al-levavekha |
| [shema-vahavta-l4](m/israeli/line/shema-vahavta-l4.mp3) | וְשִׁנַּנְתָּם לְבָנֶיךָ וְדִבַּרְתָּ בָּם בְּשִׁבְתְּךָ בְּבֵיתֶךָ וּבְלֶכְתְּךָ בַדֶּרֶךְ וּבְשָׁכְבְּךָ וּבְקוּמֶךָ׃ | and thou shalt teach them diligently unto thy children, and shalt talk of them when thou sittest in thy house, and when thou walkest by the way, and when thou liest down, and when thou risest up. | veshinantam levanekha vedibarta bam beshivtekha beveitekha uvelekhtekha vaderekh uveshakhbekha uvekumekha |
| [shema-vahavta-l5](m/israeli/line/shema-vahavta-l5.mp3) | וּקְשַׁרְתָּם לְאוֹת עַל־יָדֶךָ וְהָיוּ לְטֹטָפֹת בֵּין עֵינֶיךָ׃ | And thou shalt bind them for a sign upon thy hand, and they shall be for frontlets between thine eyes. | ukeshartam le'ot al-yadekha vehayu letotafot bein einekha |
| [shema-vahavta-l6](m/israeli/line/shema-vahavta-l6.mp3) | וּכְתַבְתָּם עַל־מְזוּזֹת בֵּיתֶךָ וּבִשְׁעָרֶיךָ׃ | And thou shalt write them upon the door-posts of thy house, and upon thy gates. | ukhetavtam al-mezuzot beitekha uvish'arekha |
| [shir-hamaalot-l1](m/israeli/line/shir-hamaalot-l1.mp3) | שִׁיר הַמַּעֲלוֹת בְּשׁוּב אֲדֹנָי אֶת־שִׁיבַת צִיּוֹן הָיִינוּ כְּחֹלְמִים׃ | A Song of Ascents. When the LORD brought back those that returned to Zion, We were like unto them that dream. | shir hama'alot beshuv adonai et-shivat tziyon hayinu kecholemim |
| [shir-hamaalot-l2](m/israeli/line/shir-hamaalot-l2.mp3) | אָז יִמָּלֵא שְׂחוֹק פִּינוּ וּלְשׁוֹנֵנוּ רִנָּה אָז יֹאמְרוּ בַגּוֹיִם הִגְדִּיל אֲדֹנָי לַעֲשׂוֹת עִם־אֵלֶּה׃ | Then was our mouth filled with laughter, and our tongue with singing; then said they among the nations: 'The LORD hath done great things with these.' | az yimale sechok pinu uleshonenu rinah az yomru vagoyim higdil adonai la'asot im-eleh |
| [shir-hamaalot-l3](m/israeli/line/shir-hamaalot-l3.mp3) | הִגְדִּיל אֲדֹנָי לַעֲשׂוֹת עִמָּנוּ הָיִינוּ שְׂמֵחִים׃ | The LORD hath done great things with us; we are rejoiced. | higdil adonai la'asot imanu hayinu semechim |
| [shir-hamaalot-l4](m/israeli/line/shir-hamaalot-l4.mp3) | שׁוּבָה אֲדֹנָי אֶת־שְׁבִיתֵנוּ כַּאֲפִיקִים בַּנֶּגֶב׃ | Turn our captivity, O LORD, as the streams in the dry land. | shuvah adonai et-shevitenu ka'afikim banegev |
| [shir-hamaalot-l5](m/israeli/line/shir-hamaalot-l5.mp3) | הַזֹּרְעִים בְּדִמְעָה בְּרִנָּה יִקְצֹרוּ׃ | They that sow in tears Shall reap in joy. | hazore'im bedim'ah berinah yiktzoru |
| [shir-hamaalot-l6](m/israeli/line/shir-hamaalot-l6.mp3) | הָלוֹךְ יֵלֵךְ וּבָכֹה נֹשֵׂא מֶשֶׁךְ־הַזָּרַע בֹּא־יָבוֹא בְרִנָּה נֹשֵׂא אֲלֻמֹּתָיו׃ | Though he goeth on his way weeping that beareth the measure of seed, He shall come home with joy, bearing his sheaves. | halokh yelekh uvakhoh nose meshekh-hazara bo-yavo verinah nose alumotav |

Bless us, our father, all of us as one, with the light of your face; for by the light of your face you gave us, LORD our God, a Torah of life, love of kindness, righteousness, blessing, mercy, life and peace.
